McLeod had failed to play a third land as well, so the decision could either be incredibly good if the trend continued, or terrible if it allowed McLeod to find one and get back into the game. McLeod did indeed find one, a [Halimar Depths](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Halimar+Depths) , and he used it to set up his next draws. It took him a couple of turns to find a fourth, but he was able to stay productive, using a [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) to drop Jace when he worked his way down to three loyalty.

Aintrazi wasn't doing anything at all. When McLeod aimed a [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe) his way, it revealed that all he had was a [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) , [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) , and a fist of lands.

"Your Jaces are *terrible*,"McLeod commented upon seeing Aintrazi's hand.

"I know, they're atrocious," Aintrazi agreed. "They give me nothing but lands!"

Aintrazi tried to make his Sphinx on the following turn, but a [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) kept it from the table. McLeod finally sifted his way through enough of his deck, and had a clear enough opening, to play his [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) . All Aintrzi could do was play a freshly-drawn Solemn Simularum and start attacking. At this point, virtually every card in McLeod's deck turned on his Ascension. Turns went by, with Aintrazi attacking for two and McLeod doing nothing. 17. 15. 13… Eventually, McLeod broke it with a [See Beyond](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=See+Beyond) , getting his first counter before ending his turn. Aintrazi cracked with his Simulacrum. A [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) to kill it threatened to give his Ascension a second counter. An [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) with kicker sent the Ascension back before it could receive it. Aintrazi drew his two cards and put the Simulacrum in the graveyard. When McLeod tried to replay the Ascension on the following turn, a [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze) sent it to the graveyard.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/26/5a/265a863768a544945211278f691592c5996eb3560e47165ab08133d77777f6e8.jpg)*Ali Aintrazi*

All of those lands Aintrazi had drawn were an impressive sight now that he had them all in play. With that much mana available to him, he had to think about how to best use it. He had *no* copies of [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit) in play yet, much to his dismay. He was forced to fight with traditional creatures, making a replacement [Solemn Simulacrum](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Solemn+Simulacrum) before passing the turn. An attempt at a second [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) was met by another [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze) from Aintrazi.

Aintrazi now had a pretty slight advantage, being behind in cards, but with an attacking creature in play. He got McLeod down to 9 with his Solemn before he sealed the deal with a [Karn Liberated](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Karn+Liberated) . McLeod tried to get *something* going with a [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) , but Aintrazi had a [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) to end the game right there.

**James McLeod 2 – Ali Aintrazi 2**

### Game Five

With the exception of the second game, which was pretty firmly in McLeod's hands the entire time, this had been an incredibly close match. Fitting that it would go to a fifth game. McLeod nearly immediately kept his hand, and Ali kept his hand after a bit of thought (and belatedly drawing his seventh card!).

The previous two games had been defined by early discard from Aintrazi, though this game went a little different. Both players played a [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) on their first turn. After resolving his, Aintrazi did a little fist pump and exclaimed, "Ha! Tar Pit," as he put the [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit) onto the table. Unfortunately…it was still the first turn and he'd already played his land to *cast* the [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) . Both players laughed as it was pointed out, and he picked it back up, admitting that he was "just happy to draw one after last game." It came into play on the next turn.

McLeod got a bead on things on his turn with a [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe) revealing a hand of juice from Aintrazi. [Karn Liberated](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Karn+Liberated) , [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan) , [Memoricide](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Memoricide) , [Praetor's Grasp](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Praetor%27s+Grasp) , [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) , [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) , and another [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit) (lucky!). What a grip! The first of those spells to see the light of play was the [Praetor's Grasp](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Praetor%27s+Grasp) , fetching a [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe) from McLeod's deck and taking a quick note on what *wasn't* in it.

Now down one card in his deck, McLeod went back to digging. [Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder) kept the status quo of his deck, though McLeod did nothing else, choosing instead to sit on [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) . Aintrazi had managed to draw lands for his last two turns, enabling him to play a land every turn, keeping pace with McLeod. Other than drawing and playing lands, neither played anything until McLeod broke the standoff with a [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) . Aintrazi countered with [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) , leaving McLeod tapped out and vulnerable to [Memoricide](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Memoricide) . Just to be safe, Aintrazi used his stolen [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe) to check the status of McLeod's hand, revealing [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) , [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) , a couple of [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) s, and a [Mental Misstep](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mental+Misstep) . With the coast clear, Aintrazi stripped McLeod of his [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) s with a [Memoricide](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Memoricide) .

Now all that was left was for Aintrazi to get a threat down, or start attacking with his [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit) s. McLeod revealed what was coming with a [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe) that revealed a [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan) in the near future. Aintrazi tried to strip a [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) from McLeod's hand with an [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) , but McLeod had a [Mental Misstep](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mental+Misstep) . When Aintrazi went for the Titan, it met the first of McLeod's [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) s. When he chose to animate his [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit) and attack, McLeod tried to fry it with a [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) , giving Aintrazi the chance to use the [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze) he'd been holding. McLeod ran out a desperate [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) , which slipped into play, but Aintrazi sent it home with an [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) with kicker.

McLeod went for the Sphinx again, and again it hit, guaranteed to draw him two more cards on Aintrazi's turn. An [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) stripped one of them, a [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) , from McLeod's hand. Knowing that the path was clear, Aintrazi made [Karn Liberated](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Karn+Liberated) , and he immediately ate the Sphinx, dropping to three loyalty. Despite being able to kill Karn, McLeod had other things in mind, aiming a kicked [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) at Aintrazi instead of Karn. When he revealed a [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) that he had found with [Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder) , it made sense. He simply wanted to make the most efficient use of the burn spells he had remaining.

And burn he did. After getting [Duress](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Duress) ed down to a lone [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island) , McLeod drew a [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) and aimed it right at Aintrazi, dropping him to ten. Unfortunately, the burn stopped coming after that, leaving Aintrazi free to swing him to death with his [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit) , winning an incredibly tight match and securing a spot in the semifinals.

**James McLeod 2 – Ali Aintrazi 3**

![](https://i.ormos.uk/2d/4f/2d4f48fe30a8cddbe8d108ed25a7e000b3d9e885c9a04b08b73e6656dbd1c9ff.jpg)*Ali, Victorious*

## U.S. Nationals Quarterfinals – Haibing Hu (CawBlade) vs. Owen Turtenwald (CawBlade)

*by Brian David-Marshall* 

Haibing Hu has likely been playing longer than anyone else in this Top 8 with his first Pro Tour experience going all the way back to Pro Tour Nice. He had played the CawBlade deck previous to the recent bannings and used it to qualify for Pro Tour Philadelphia but was not planning on playing the deck this weekend. The version he ended up playing was designed by Gerry Thompson and was essentially handed to him in his drive to the event. After playing what he described as a sloppy round one of the event, he realized that his opportunities to play **Magic** competitively were precious and told himself to tighten up. And that is exactly what he has done.

His opponent, Owen Turtenwald, has been playing tight all year with a Top 16 at PT Paris and a slew of Grand Prix Top 8s propelling him to near the top of the Player of the Year standings. Owen was playing the same list as fellow Top 8 competitors LSV and Ochoa with just minor differences. With 10 Pro Points awaiting the winner of this event, and the possibility of the extra points available to the National team, winning this match could go a long way toward his Player of the Year aspirations.

### Game One

Fittingly for this mirror match both players kicked things off with [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) -- a card that saw every single deck in the Top 8 play the full four copies. Haibing -- who had gotten to play first by virtue of higher standing in the Swiss rounds -- followed up with [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) on turn two.

Owen dug with a second [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) and passed the turn. Hu untapped to play [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+Feast+and+Famine) and attacked for one. Owen played a [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) a turn later and searched out a second one. It chump blocked Hu's equipped [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) and Haibing reinforced with a second copy. Owen played out another [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) and searched the balance out of his deck

Hu played out third [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) to round out his quartet as well. Then he played [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) and swung with his two [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)s -- Owen threw a [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) in the path of the oncoming [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine) bearer. Owen played a tapped land and passed the turn with a lone [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk). Hu attacked and Owen chumped. He played [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) on the [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine) at the end of the turn. Hu used that opportunity to [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) Owen's Colonnade.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/21/e4/21e4221aa9f97d2ddbc0ba89a3370831ad899c01334cb664f9312002c00b09f7.jpg)

The [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)s kept coming for Owen as did a fresh copy of [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) . Hu untapped -- and just sent his two [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)s in. Owen blocked the [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine)ed one and used [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) to kill Hu's [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) by bouncing the [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine) at the end of the turn. Hu replayed the [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine) on his next turn and paid three more mana when Owen [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) ed it. Hu did not have mana left over to requip but attacked for one.

[Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) showed up to help Owen and promptly killed the tapped [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk). Owen still had one mana up -- representing [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember) if Hu wanted to animate his land and attack it. Hu played [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) and made a token with [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) , which he promptly used on Owen's Collonade. The score was 14 to 20 in Hu's favor.

[Timely Reinforcements](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Timely+Reinforcements) arrived for Owen. He broke an [Arid Mesa](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Arid+Mesa) and and went down to 19 -- he had gone up to 20 from the spell. He tapped out to play [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine) and then went +2 for the Gideon. Hu moved the sword to a bird token and Gideon died -- but no discard or untap for Hu since he attacked the Planeswalker. Hu played another [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk).

Owen did not equip and just sent in the team of tokens. Hu had no blocks. Owen tapped all his mana and flipped a [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) on the board. Hu looked at his hand but had to let Owen draw two cards. Ironically the culprit he drew was [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) . Hu animated his land and sent everything toward the red zone. Owen put the Sphinx in he way of the sworded bird token and Hu played [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember) . [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) after combat gave him a fresh token.

Owen played another [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine) and Hu let it resolve. Owen equipped two soldiers -- and maybe Hu rethought not using the [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) . He discarded it to an unblocked token and chumped the other. Hu fanned through Owen graveyard and then attacked with everything. Owen [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) ed the equipped bird with kicker. Owen fell to 7. Hu had to think... He opted to play another [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) over Day of Judgment. and played a fetch land.

Owen [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) d Hu's Colonnade and Hu did it back to a [Seachrome Coast](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Seachrome+Coast) . Hu had two tokens back to block with the potential for another.

Owen played [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) and attacked with two sworded tokens. Hu chumped them both and fetched land at end of turn. Owen had three mana untapped and three cards in hand. Hu had just one card -- the aforementioned Day of Judgement. Hu put the [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine) on a [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) and attacked with everything.

"You got it," said Owen.

### Game Two

[Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) led off on both sides with turn two [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) for Owen. No [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) for Hu who passed turn with two mana open. Owen played a second [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) while Hu played a pair of [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) s on his second turn. Owen's turn four [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) was more than enough for Hu to pull the trigger on Day of Judgment on his turn four.

Owen started to rebuild with Gideon which he promptly sent to 8 loyalty while Hu had [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) . Owen also had [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) as well as [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember) for Hu's. Hu had his own copy of [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember) and then played another Angel -- it is the last one that always gets you -- and passed the turn with it and a bird token.

Owen dug for cards with [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) and took advantage of Hu's tapped mana to use [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) on Hu's copy of the same card. Owen played a [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) and passed the turn with Gideon sitting on 11 loyalty.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/20/ff/20ff92f545615fac0e8b27336ecb58e015f6bdb3a2b31b79e9abef48ba2e9bcb.jpg)*Owen Turtenwald*

[Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) from Hu found [Scalding Tarn](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Scalding+Tarn) . He attacked as he was contractually obligated to do and Owen traded a bird token and [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) for two bird tokens. Gideon fell to 8. Hu made two tokens, played [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) , and drew a card. Gideon went to 6 to kill the Angel and Owen played a [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) -- he had four mana open including [Inkmoth Nexus](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inkmoth+Nexus) .

Hu drew a card with Jace. He sent one token at Gideon and Owen traded his [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) for it. Owen kicked [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) at the end of the turn and Hu played [Spell Pierce](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Pierce) after confirming that Owen was on one card in hand. Owen played [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) , played Tarn and broke it -- "Three in hand?" he asked of Hu -- before animating his Colonnade and his Gideon. He sent the Planeswalker at Hu and the land at Jace. Hu traded Colonnades and chumped the Gideon.

Hu untapped to play [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) and [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) the Planeswalker. Owen had one card and played it -- [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+Feast+and+Famine) . He animated his Inkmoth and equipped. Owen wanted to get Hu to use the Edge to clear the way for his Colonnade. Hu took three poison and discarded an [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island) . Owen untapped but had no cards.

[Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) showed up for Hu and he promptly played a second one -- they are like buses in that regard. Owen attempted another [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) but it was foiled by [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) . Hu attacked for two with his [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)s and the rest of the fleet came down post combat. Owen equipped his Nexus again and this time Hu used his [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) on it -- he had drawn a [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember) . Hu attacked for 8 with his full suite of [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)s and a Colonnade. When Owen saw the [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember) after tapping all his mana to animate equip his Colonnade they were onto game three with the bricks of the wall pressed into Owen's back.

### Game Three

Owen kept a six carder and a turn two [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) undid any damage from his mulligan as he searched out all three of his remaining birds. He did not play [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) on turn two, rather an [Azure Mage](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Azure+Mage) that can do a little of everything from beat down to control. Hu promptly cracked a fetch and [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) ed it. Owen played a second [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) on turn three but frowned when Hu played [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) -- he had the [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember) though. The third [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) came down but Hu had yet another [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) -- Owen's draw seemed land heavy.

Hu looked at the six lands across from him and shrugged. He played Gideon and had it [Spell Pierce](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Pierce) d. He did have a sixth land to play for his [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) -- something made more frustrating when Owen used [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) on Hu's [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) and said go. Hu had a [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+Feast+and+Famine) [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) ed but resolved his next copy a turn later -- making bird tokens all the while. Owen used [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) with kicker on the [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) and sent in his [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)s -- Hu traded a bird for it.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/9d/fe/9dfe59de7fe2f2cabda509110498eec936e9e80c4248d0d0bebb66aab4412d57.jpg)*Haibing Hu*

Hu equipped a bird token and attacked. Owen threw a [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) in the way. Meanwhile Hu was now fetching [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)s of his own. [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) and land gave Owen some breathing room as well as a clear window for Hu to replay his own Angel after Owen chumped the sworded token. Hu took four from Owen's Colonnade but was able to resolve [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) .

Hu was drawing cards while Owen was playing off the top of his deck. He played a [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) and chose not attack. Two more cards came his way during Owen's turn. Hu played [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) with kicker on his opponent's [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) when Owen said go. Owen broke the fetch he had just played for a fourth token to go with his [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk).

Hu played a land and sent all his his non-angels and non-sphinx creatures into battle. When dust settled Owen had only a [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk). Hu played [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) and then went to his discard step. Owen played [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) with kicker on the Sphinx. He still had five mana open. Hu played [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) and two Spell pierce to fight through it -- he would get two cards back on Owen's draw step.

"Discard?" asked Owen.

"Not any more," said Hu.

Owen replayed the Angel and played a land his hand was empty. Hu [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember) ed the Angel and attacked with everyone. Owen chumped as best he could but it would require a miracle to come back with and empty grip and board against Hu's full hand and air force. Just to be safe...Hu played Gideon as well.

**Final result: Haibing Hu advances to the semifinals in a three game sweep of Owen Turtenwald.**

## U.S. Nationals Semifinals – Ali Aintrazi vs. Haibing Hu

*by Nate Price* 

This semifinals match showcased a couple of things that I was excited to see. First, there was the obvious inclusion of Ali Aintrazi's UB deck, the only non-CawBlade deck left in the Top 8 field. Despite the resurgence and success of non-CawBlade decks in the Standard portion of the tournament (it only made up four of the eleven top decks), *six* copies piloted by some of the more prominent names in American **Magic** made the Top 8 due to the mixed-format nature of Nationals. Getting something that didn't put a Sword on a Bird was refreshing. Also, Haibing Hu, though he was playing a CawBlade deck hiself, was running a version with a few interesting card choices in both the maindeck and sideboard.

"Sweet, a deck I don't have dead cards against for game 1," Aintrazi joked as he sat down to the table.

"Yeah, most people do against him," Hu said, nodding his head.

### Game One

Hu started things off with a second-turn [Squadron \<a class="autocard-link" data-image-url="http:&gatherer.wizards.com&Handlers&Image.ashx?type=card&name=Squadron+Hawk" href="http:&gatherer.wizards.com&Pages&Card&Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk">Squadron Hawk<&a>](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+%3Ca+class%3D%22autocard-link%22+data-image-url%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fgatherer.wizards.com%2FHandlers%2FImage.ashx%3Ftype%3Dcard%26amp%3Bname%3DSquadron%2BHawk%22+href%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fgatherer.wizards.com%2FPages%2FCard%2FDetails.aspx%3F%26amp%3Bname%3DSquadron%252BHawk%22%3ESquadron+Hawk%3C%2Fa%3E) . The little birdie fetched up a few more copies, filling Hu's hand. Aintrazi answered with an [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) , taking [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) from a hand with [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember) , [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) , [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) , and three [Squadron \<a class="autocard-link" data-image-url="http:&gatherer.wizards.com&Handlers&Image.ashx?type=card&name=Squadron+Hawk" href="http:&gatherer.wizards.com&Pages&Card&Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk">Squadron Hawk<&a>](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+%3Ca+class%3D%22autocard-link%22+data-image-url%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fgatherer.wizards.com%2FHandlers%2FImage.ashx%3Ftype%3Dcard%26amp%3Bname%3DSquadron%2BHawk%22+href%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fgatherer.wizards.com%2FPages%2FCard%2FDetails.aspx%3F%26amp%3Bname%3DSquadron%252BHawk%22%3ESquadron+Hawk%3C%2Fa%3E) s. On his turn, his [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)s started to take over the skies. The first started attacking while the second swooped into play. Aintrazi played a [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) of his own, using his second ability to take him out of danger, but giving Hu a card in the process. Hu added a third [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) to his team and attacked with the other two, knocking Jace to three. Aintrazi realized he was done with his Jace, using it the second time to draw only himself a card. He also made a [Solemn Simulacrum](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Solemn+Simulacrum) that resolved after a bit of thought from Hu.

On his turn, Hu went into thought. After a [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) , he sent two of his three [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)s at Jace to finish him off and the last at Aintrazi. After combat, the final [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk) joined the skies. Aintrazi popped back with his Simulacrum, dropping Hu to 17. He made a second robot after combat, giving him a slightly better board, and a significant mana advantage. With so many lands in play, he felt content to [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) Hu's [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) , dropping him to four lands. At the end of Aintrazi's turn, Hu paid the full amount of life to [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember) the newest robot.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/3b/18/3b189bd6621f5fbaf036f149933b19868e6444b6d281bda4aae8d24a0c1ff4f7.jpg)*'On his turn, Hu went into thought...'*

Hu's [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) to start his turn put him into the tank. He eventually decided to swap both of the cards for a better one off of his deck. At this point, his Squadron of [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)s started taking four-point chunks out of Aintrazi's life total. Aintrazi was giving back, swinging with his Simulacrum. He tried to get in with his [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit) on one turn, but Hu used [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) to kill it. When Aintrazi played a second Tar Pit, Hu matched it with a second Edge. With one attack, Hu dropped Aintrazi to 2 life, while sitting at 7 himself. Aintrazi attacked with his Simulacrum and passed the turn. When Hu sent his Squad on the following turn, Aintrazi conceded.

**Ali Aintrazi 0 – Haibing Hu 1**

### Game Two

Hu started the second game with a pair of mulligans on the draw, which had to have Aintrazi feeling good about his chances this game. Hu made an effort to get back the cards he'd lost with a third-turn [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) , but Aintrazi had an [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) to cap it at one card, as well as a Jace of his own to take the place of Hu's. Hu thought for a moment about his play before deciding to trap Aintrazi's Jace in an [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) , trying to pave the way for his own. Aintrazi tried to prevent this with an [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) , but Hu had a [Mental Misstep](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mental+Misstep) . After untapping, Hu made a [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) , drawing two cards and passing the turn. Hu only had access to one mana, so Aintrazi took the opportunity to cast a [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) . Hu had nothing to say about it, and nothing to do either, letting Aintrazi draw a pair of cards not only during his draw step, but also off of a Jace activation. Hu used the rest of his turn to make two [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) s.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/07/cb/07cb1c49e1c23a57a13e459e33c7105c8d2b3844563a6dfd29c39aafcb69de09.jpg)*Haibing Hu*

With a hand full of cards and a severe advantage, Aintrazi began his turn. His [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) met a [Mental Misstep](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mental+Misstep) from Hu, but his [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) locked Hu back down at three lands. Aintrazi attacked and then passed the turn with four lands up, content to coast on the wings (and cards) of his [Consecrated Sphinxk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Consecrated%2BSphinxk). Hu decided that since Aintrazi was already drawing a bunch of cards, he might as well just use Jace's +2 ability, keeping him from going away. An [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) revealed a pair of [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) s, a [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) of his own, a pair of [Spell Pierce](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Pierce) s, and a [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+Feast+and+Famine) . Aintrazi took the Sword, unafraid of the [Spell Pierce](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Pierce) s thanks to his severe mana advantage.

He had an interesting decision to make on his turn. If he attacked with his [Consecrated Sphinxk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Consecrated%2BSphinxk), and Hu drew a fifth land, Gideon would come down and kill it. But if he didn't attack, Hu would get two draws, one for his turn and one for Jace, to try to find it. Ultimately, he chose to cast [Black Sun's Zenith](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Black+Sun%27s+Zenith) for one to kill the [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)s, attack Jace, and [Despise](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Despise) a copy of Gideon from Hu's hand.

While sitting at 7 life, Hu managed to find a [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment) to kill the [Consecrated Sphinxk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Consecrated%2BSphinxk). Aintrazi replaced it, but Hu found a fifth land, playing [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) and forcing Aintrazi to attack him. Aintrazi did so, animating his [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit) , as well, to knock Gideon to one loyalty. On the following attack, Hu was dead. Aintrazi had drawn well over a dozen cards off his [Consecrated Sphinxk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Consecrated%2BSphinxk), and that crushing advantage combined with a severe shortage of lands thanks to his mulligans was enough to do Hu in.

**Ali Aintrazi 1 – Haibing Hu 1**

![](https://i.ormos.uk/2a/f8/2af86775fda0a57374cddb54b7adebb5062e95058e2efb81e032730d3c2f29b4.jpg)

### Game Three

Aintrazi's attempt at a first-turn Inquisition was stopped by a [Spell Pierce](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Pierce) , and the one that followed two turns later was countered with [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) . When Hu tried to make a [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+Feast+and+Famine) afterwards, Aintrazi stopped that with a [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) of his own. To this point in the game, the story was more what hadn't happened than what had.

The first thing *to* happen was a [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) from Hu on turn five. Aintrazi kicked an [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) at it and filled the space with a [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) . Hu returned the favor of the previous turn, kicking an [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) on the [Consecrated Sphinxk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Consecrated%2BSphinxk) during his upkeep. Aintrazi replayed it on his turn, and Hu once again cast [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) . Before Aintrazi cast it for the third time, he cast [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) , stealing an [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) from a hand with [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+Feast+and+Famine) , [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) , [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment) , and [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) . The [Consecrated Sphinxk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Consecrated%2BSphinxk) hit the table for the *third* time, and this time it actually got to draw Aintrazi a pair of cards. Hu used his [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment) to clear it away, but Aintrazi was holding a second copy to replace it. Hu made a [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) , using it to force Aintrazi to attack. He had a [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) in reserve, just in case Aintrazi tried the same maneuver as the last game and animated his Tar Pit.

Aintrazi got a glimpse of the lone [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) in Hu's hand with [Despise](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Despise) before dropping the bomb and stealing Gideon with a [Volition Reins](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Volition+Reins) . At this point, Aintrazi had a *severe* advantage, especially considering the four cards in his hand. Hu had gotten a [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+Feast+and+Famine) into play alongside his Gideon, but he now had no creatures to hand it to. He drew his card and passed the turn. Aintrazi locked things up with a [Liliana Vess](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Liliana+Vess) , hitting the last card in Hu's hand, before swinging his team at Hu. One more turn and it was over.

**Ali Aintrazi 2 – Haibing Hu 1**

![](https://i.ormos.uk/87/a7/87a7cc420b06b5f2c0804d3d608b3e79b237f3e9ec2d8133abfa0a96005f0d69.jpg)*Ali Aintrazi*

### Game Four

Aintrazi managed to resolve a first-turn [Despise](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Despise) , revealing two copies of [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment) , [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) , [Glacial Fortress](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Glacial+Fortress) , [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) , and [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) , which he was forced to take. As though he wouldn't anyway. That made things clear for him to play a Jace of his own on the third turn, though he only got a single card out of it before Hu trapped him under [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) . Hu then took the Planeswalker advantage with [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) , which caused Aintrazi to kick it back to Hu's hand with an [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) . Rather than try for it once more, while Aintrazi had four mana open, Hu opted to pass the turn. Aintrazi took advantage of this window to cast his threat: the [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) that had won him his last two games. Hu had an [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) to kick at it during the end of turn, putting himself on the right side of the tempo this time around. He even used a [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) to put Aintrazi back down to five lands, but Aintrazi had the sixth and was able to recast the [Consecrated Sphinxk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Consecrated%2BSphinxk). He drew two cards off it before Day wiped it clear.

Aintrazi went after Hu's hand with an [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) , but just ended up looking at [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) , [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) , [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment) , and a [Seachrome Coast](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Seachrome+Coast) . Hu hadn't played the Coast on his last turn, and was dropped to three lands when Aintrazi used two [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) s to kill his lands. He also added a [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan) to the board, but Hu got to untap his new Coast and cast [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment) to wipe it all away. An [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) and a land came down for Hu, giving him a little birdie, but Aintrazi had the [Doom Blade](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Doom+Blade) for the Angel. He had another copy when Hu tried to make a [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) . At this point, the attacking began. Hu sent for one with his bird, while Aintrazi sent a pair of [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit) s after Hu. At two life, and facing lethal damage on the following turn, Hu made a [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) . Aintrazi sent his two lands, dropping him to two loyalty. Hu finally found a [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) , playing all four of his [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)s in one turn before Aintrazi killed Gideon. Hu had a single turn left. He drew his card, surveyed the board, and shook Aintrazi's hand, sending him off to the finals and ensuring him a spot on the U.S. National team!

**Ali Aintrazi 3 – Haibing Hu 1**

## U.S. Nationals Semifinals – David Ochoa vs. Brandon Nelson

*by Steve Sadin* 

This time last year, David Ochoa was playing in the Top 4 of U.S. Nationals. Unfortunately for Ochoa, he lost his Semifinals match, and his 3/4 playoff match to end the tournament in fourth place. While this was still an excellent finish, Ochoa was understandably disappointed that he wasn't able to represent the United States as a part of the National Team at Worlds.

Today, David Ochoa again finds himself in the Top 4 of U.S. Nationals, and again needs to win (at least) one of his next two matches to earn a slot on the U.S. National Team. Will Ochoa find redemption this year, or will he again be forced to watch the team competition at Worlds from the sideline as the U.S. Team's "alternate".

Brandon Nelson is a veteran of the Midwest grinder scene, who has the ability to qualify for events seemingly at will, but has (up until this point), been unable to get the finishes necessary to keep himself from going back the PTQ circuit to earn his invitations.

Nelson qualified for the first two Pro Tours of the season via PTQs and came into this weekend with 8 Pro Points on the year. Consequently, Nelson has a lot more at stake here than "just" a spot on the U.S. National Team, as a win here would put Nelson on (at least) 16 Pro Points for the season – bumping himself up to Level 3 in the Pro Player's Club, earning himself an invitation to Pro Tour Philadelphia, and will need to pick up only the bare minimum two points from attending Philadelphia and Worlds to insure himself Level 4 status, and the automatic qualification for every Pro Tour that comes with it, for the remainder of this year, and next year.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/1e/61/1e61b3a4774e23048ba1750d5b3b49f176174cb84dac756d48a83aae62f5606e.jpg)

### Game One

Ochoa mulliganed to six on the play, but was able to refill his hand when he resolved a turn two [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) fetching up three more copies of the format defining two drop. Ochoa then stuck a second [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) on turn two, but he had no third land to go with it.

Nelson cast a [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) on his third turn which he ticked up to 5 counters – this both insured that his planeswalker would live through Ochoa's attack, and let the mana-light Ochoa draw into a third land.

Rather than cast a third [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) , Ochoa (no doubt respecting the possibility of [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment) ) activated his [Inkmoth Nexus](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inkmoth+Nexus) and attacked [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) down to two counters.

Nelson's [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) got bounced by a kicked [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) , allowing Ochoa to kill his opponent's [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) with his attack.

The players then went back and forth playing lands, and hawks, for a number of turns – but the formerly mana-starved Ochoa drew several more lands than his opponent giving himself a huge advantage going into the late game.

Ochoa waited until he had seven lands in play before casting his first non- [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) , non- [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) Spell of the game, a [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) . Nelson used two [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) s to counter the flier, untapped, and passed the turn back to Ochoa.

Ochoa, safe in the knowledge that Nelson had already used two of his three maindeck [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) s (the players were given a chance to review each other's decklists before the match) cast a [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) which got bounced by an [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) .

Ochoa recast his [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) , but this time Nelson was ready with a [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment) .

[Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) followed by [Arid Mesa](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Arid+Mesa) gave Ochoa five power worth of fliers, and left him with a bunch of untapped mana to discourage Nelson from trying anything too impressive. Nelson simply resolved a [Timely Reinforcements](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Timely+Reinforcements) and passed the turn.

A [Scalding Tarn](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Scalding+Tarn) gave Ochoa two more fliers, and when he tried for a [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) , Nelson attempted to counter it with a [Spell Pierce](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Pierce) . Ochoa, who had five additional mana available, thought for a while before ultimately allowing his planeswalker to get countered – feeling content to continue riding his fliers to victory.

An [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) bounced Ochoa's [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) , and after combat Ochoa again attempted to resolve a [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) . Nelson once again attempted to counter it with a [Spell Pierce](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Pierce) , but this time Ochoa had no problem paying the two mana to make sure his planeswalker would resolve as it set up a lethal attack a turn later.

**David Ochoa 1 – Brandon Nelson 0**

![](https://i.ormos.uk/68/b5/68b54b71b1bd851cd202793895abbe0bc06f8e4c8f26d474978e98530e765c13.jpg)

### Game Two

Ochoa started game two with a mulligan, but made the first play of the game when he cast a [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) on his third turn. Nelson tried to up the ante with a [Timely Reinforcements](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Timely+Reinforcements) , but it got countered by Ochoa's [Spell Pierce](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Pierce) .

Ochoa missed his fourth land drop, but kept the pressure on by adding a [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+Feast+and+Famine) to his side of the board. Nelson laid a fifth land and calmly passed the turn. When Ochoa equipped [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+Feast+and+Famine) to his [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) , Nelson was ready with an [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) bouncing the artifact.

A fourth land allowed Ochoa to cast a second [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) post-combat, and Nelson again passed his turn with no play despite having enough lands to cast every spell in his deck.

When Ochoa added his third [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) to the board, Nelson decided that he had had enough, and wiped the board with a [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment) .

Ochoa's [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) got countered by [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) , and with only a single white mana available, Ochoa could do nothing to stop Nelson's [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) from hitting play.

Ochoa spent his turn re-deploying his [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+Feast+and+Famine) , and summoning his final [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) . Nelson used a [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember) to take out the hawk before casting a [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+Feast+and+Famine) of his own which he equipped to his animated [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) .

While Ochoa was able to briefly get his opponent's [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) off the board, he was never able to come up with a permanent answer for the planeswalker, and wound up dying to a combination of [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) , and [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) a few turns later.

**David Ochoa 1 – Brandon Nelson 1**

![](https://i.ormos.uk/97/ca/97caeedb0f8c4ece1e49cafe4bfadd19d83590f9ec1e97f614b3ee2da3f23e8a.jpg)*David Ochoa*

### Game Three

Ochoa got things started with a [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) followed by a [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+Feast+and+Famine) , while Nelson cast a couple of Hawks of his own.

On his fourth turn Ochoa moved to equip his sword, and [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) ed Nelson's [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember) – forcing Nelson to chump block with one of his [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) s.

Nelson's [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) took out the sword, but it also left him tapped out – giving Ochoa an opportunity to resolve something big. Ochoa missed his fifth land drop, so instead of slapping a [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) onto the board, he had to spend his turn casting a [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) (which he found a land off of) and two more [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) s.

Ochoa cast a [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) , and used a [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) to counter Nelson's [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) .

With Ochoa tapped out, Nelson cast and equipped a [Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+Feast+and+Famine) to force a block. Ochoa's [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) got countered by [Deprive](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Deprive) , but the [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) that Ochoa cast on his next turn resolved – giving himself a steady stream of blockers for his opponent's sword wielding [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) .

Nelson drew a [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) to stop Ochoa from drawing any more additional cards, but Ochoa's replacement planeswalker, a [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) , was able to hit the board.

Nelson's first attempt at [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) got countered, but his second attempt to bounce [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) resolved. After bouncing the planeswalker, Nelson attacked in with his equipped bird. Ochoa activated a [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) , which got killed by a [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember) , forcing Ochoa to continue chump blocking with his [Emeria Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Emeria+Angel) tokens.

Nelson's [Sun Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sun+Titan) brought back a [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) , but Ochoa wiped the board clean of creatures with a [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment) , and attacked in with his [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) . On a mere 2 life, Nelson had to chump block the 6/6 Gideon with an [Inkmoth Nexus](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inkmoth+Nexus) .

A post-combat [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) gave Ochoa a lot of protection, as it prevented Nelson from being able to kill [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) with an attack from [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) .

While Nelson was able to stay alive for a few turns, by chump blocking with his [Inkmoth Nexus](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inkmoth+Nexus) es, and [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20160504013952/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) s, he didn't draw anything that could deal with his opponent's GIdeon Jura, and consequently found himself with his back against the wall going into game four.

**David Ochoa 2 – Brandon Nelson 1**

![](https://i.ormos.uk/1e/61/1e61b3a4774e23048ba1750d5b3b49f176174cb84dac756d48a83aae62f5606e.jpg)

### Game Four
